# Staging Log

## Append Protocol

- Append new package entries immediately above the unique `STAGING_LOG_APPEND_POINT` marker.
- Keep entries compact and one-package-per-section so future updates can be added quickly.
- Every new package entry must include `Canonical stage:`. Payments-runtime/admin/checkout backfill entries after B3ai should use `H##` or a true `A3*`/`A4*`/`A5*` label, not `B3*`.
- Do not run markdownlint on this scratchpad session log.

Do not infer B3 completion from the historical `B3*` payments labels.

## Stage Boundary Gate

Before declaring a canonical stage exited, run a standalone spec-conformance review against the accumulated branch state, not just the latest diff. The gate must cover the relevant `design-spec.md` stage section, `bc-manifest.md` dispositions for touched surfaces, implementation-plan gates and invariants, harness/browser evidence, Bucket-C/E preservation, perf evidence, and unresolved roadmap obligations. Record the reviewer, scope, evidence, and verdict in this staging log.

### 2026-06-17 B3z Plugin Handoff Provider Tracking

- Scope: Backtrack the real local WooPayments plugin-to-native soft cutover path, keep the target store native-owned after handoff, and fix the Core provider-state tracking warning exposed by the handoff browser/log gate.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Admin/Settings/Payments.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Admin/Settings/PaymentsTest.php`.
- Root cause: native WooPayments correctly preserves REST/Core ownership as `plugin.slug=woocommerce`, but Settings Payments provider-state snapshots and Tracks identity were still keyed by that provider plugin slug. That collided with ordinary Core providers and let stale `woocommerce` snapshots match provider rows without `_suggestion_id`, causing a PHP warning. The fix resolves the canonical snapshot/tracking key through the official extension suggestion plugin slug (`woocommerce-payments`) while leaving the REST payload ownership untouched.
- Browser/e2e evidence: target plugin-active Settings Payments loaded with one WooPayments provider row and empty cutover preflight; nonce-protected soft cutover returned target to native ownership and inactive plugin state; native Settings Payments then loaded one Test account WooPayments row with provider POST 200; target classic checkout preserved `Card`, Core card icons, `+ 3`, copyable test-card instructions, and Stripe session 200; target Blocks checkout preserved Test Mode badge/text, `Card`, Core card icons, `+ 2`, copyable test-card instructions, and Stripe session 200. The stale Blocks live-region text was also present on the reference checkout, so it is not a target-only native regression.
- Gates passed: focused RED/GREEN covered the missing `woocommerce-payments` snapshot key and stale Core-slug `_suggestion_id` warning; final related PHPUnit passed (267 tests, 1165 assertions); PHPStan for `Settings/Payments.php`, PHP syntax checks, `lint:php:changes`, `git diff --check`, and post-commit `lint:changes:branch` passed. Branch JS lint reported only the existing 34 ignored-file warnings. Restored cross-store harness passed 7/7 with reference order 405 and target order 85. Fresh Docker log scans found no new PHP notices/warnings/fatals/stack traces; target debug-log mtime stayed at the stale 2026-06-16 21:20:50 UTC plugin-active warning window.
- Review gate: Dirac (`code-reviewer`) reviewed the scoped diff read-only and approved with critical 0, high 0, medium 0.

### 2026-06-17 B3aa Settings Payments Native Onboarding API Handoff

- Scope: Restore the generic WooCommerce > Settings > Payments provider list in native WooPayments mode by removing plugin-only onboarding route assumptions, wiring native onboarding/recommendation APIs through Core-owned services, and preserving checkout test-mode shopper details.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Admin/Settings/PaymentsProviders/WooPayments/WooPaymentsService.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/NativeWooPaymentsGateway.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsHttpClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsHttpClientInterface.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsAccountService.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Admin/Settings/PaymentsProviders/WooPayments/WooPaymentsServiceTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/NativeWooPaymentsGatewayTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClientTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/FakeWooPaymentsHttpClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsAccountServiceTest.php`.
- Root cause: Target Settings Payments failure was caused by native Core regressions, not an inherent `store8889.localhost:8889` limitation. Native onboarding fields used the wrong WPCOM API shape and still depended on plugin-owned REST routes, native recommendations were not exposed through the gateway/provider contract, local wpcom-local routing was bypassed by a hardcoded public API URL, plugin-absent native fields missed `__locale` plus `available_countries`, and native account handoff could leave Core without usable cached account state or with gateway `test_mode` still pointing at a stale test key after live KYC.
- Browser/e2e evidence: target Settings Payments renders `Accept payments with Woo` with `Test account`, `Official`, `Manage`, `Activate payments`, PayPal, and offline providers; provider POST returned 200 with target providers `woocommerce_payments`, `_wc_pes_paypal_full_stack`, and `_wc_offline_payment_methods_group`, Core-owned `plugin.slug=woocommerce`, `state.test_mode=true`, `onboarding.type=native_in_context`, and `test_drive_account=true`. Reference provider POST returned 200 with extra custom gateways and plugin-owned `plugin.slug=woocommerce-payments`. Target Blocks checkout preserved `Test Mode`, card icons, `Use test card`, copy-to-clipboard test number, testing guide, and Stripe iframe. ### 2026-06-17 B3ab Mandatory WooPayments Cutover Hardening

- Scope: Split manual and mandatory WooPayments plugin-to-native deactivation semantics so the one-click disable action remains capability-gated, while the default-off mandatory merged-feature phase can auto-deactivate the plugin after native preflight without depending on current-user plugin capabilities; preserve `WC_ALLOW_MERGED_FEATURE_PLUGINS` as the parallel-testing bypass.

- Root cause: The mandatory auto-deactivation path reused `disable_woopayments_plugin()`, which is correct for a human-triggered manual button but weaker than the `Packages::deactivate_merged_packages()` merged-feature precedent because it requires current-user cutover capabilities on the update/admin request. Target classic checkout preserved Card + 3, card icons, test-card copy UI, testing guide, save-to-account, and Stripe iframe/session 200.

### 2026-06-17 N7a Financial Reconciliation Gate Hardening

- Canonical stage: N7a verification coverage hardening for the A3/A5 money path; supports implementation-plan §0.6 invariant 5 before A4/A5 progression.
- Scope: Widen financial reconciliation from refund-only claims to raw Stripe charge amount/currency, capture state, refund totals and IDs, fee/net, multi-currency exchange-rate parity, dispute/payout state when present, plus native refund provider-linkage parity discovered by the widened gate.
- Source: `tools/woopayments-merge/financial-reconcile.sh`; `tools/woopayments-merge/financial-reconcile-normalize.py`; `tools/woopayments-merge/flow-drive.sh`; `tools/woopayments-merge/flow-drive-refund.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapter.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/PaymentProcessingService.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClientTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/PaymentProcessingServiceTest.php`; harness fixture tests under `tools/woopayments-merge/tests/financial-reconcile-fixtures.sh`.
- Root cause fixed: the native refund transport was posted to `refunds/<charge_id>` while the WCPay V1 endpoint expects `POST refunds` with a `charge` body parameter, and the native refund success path did not persist provider refund metadata back to the local WC refund row. Native now sends the correct refund route/body. The WooPayments adapter supplies WooPayments-specific `refund_meta`, `order_meta`, and `refund_note` payloads, while the generic `PaymentProcessingService` only matches the WC refund row and applies provider-supplied meta/note instructions.
- Gates passed: financial comparator fixtures passed for charge amount, captured amount, full/partial refunds, missing provider refund IDs, fee/net, dispute IDs, payout IDs, exchange-rate equality, and settlement-currency fee/net conversion; focused related PHPUnit `WooPaymentsApiClientTest|PaymentProcessingServiceTest|WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest` passed (71 tests, 390 assertions after the provider-neutral refactor); PHP syntax passed for touched WC PHP and harness refund driver; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:php:changes`, `git diff --check`, and PHPStan for the touched production payment classes passed.
- Runtime evidence: target native partial order 152 (`ch_3TjJeoJd67Ti1EoI1pGEQBHX`, refund `re_3TjJeoJd67Ti1EoI1QADWS9K`), target native full order 154 (`ch_3TjJfzJd67Ti1EoI1g9W2blX`, refund `re_3TjJfzJd67Ti1EoI1br2vZ7S`), and post-refactor target native partial order 156 (`ch_3TjJlOJd67Ti1EoI1r9gwQA8`, refund `re_3TjJlOJd67Ti1EoI1JJQ95nW`) reconciled against connected account `acct_1TjCnhJd67Ti1EoI`. Reference partial/full orders 467/469 reconciled against connected account `acct_1TjENrJAuuJ9nlT3`. These runs passed the widened raw-source financial matrix for charge amount, capture state, refund totals, provider refund IDs, fee/net, and absent multi-currency/dispute/payout state.
- Remaining N7a gaps: deterministic coverage is still not complete for auth/capture, dispute outcome ingestion, payout state linkage, and multi-currency target money paths. Reference dispute order 464 remains a valid fail-closed signal until provider dispute state is delivered and persisted. Target native Test Lab still cannot simulate all money paths, so the harness needs native deterministic drivers before N7a can be closed as full-matrix complete.
- Constraints: WPCOM remained untouched/read-only; no WPCOM sandbox access occurred; Stripe CLI use was read-only raw-source verification; no push was attempted; scratchpad and harness changes remain local ignored working state.

### 2026-06-17 N7b Platform Payment Method Subscription Checkout Closure

- Canonical stage: N7b Bucket-C WC Subscriptions checkout and renewal conformance, with A3 checkout request-contract parity for platform-owned WooPayments payment methods.
- Scope: Close the target native subscription checkout failure for platform-scoped card payment methods when network saved cards/platform checkout are enabled, then run the real reference-vs-target WC Subscriptions renewal compare gate using browser-created subscriptions.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/NativeWooPaymentsGateway.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapter.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsPlatformPaymentMethodContext.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/client/blocks/assets/js/extensions/payment-methods/woopayments/index.js`; `tools/woopayments-merge/subscriptions-renewal-drive.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/NativeWooPaymentsGatewayTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/client/blocks/assets/js/extensions/payment-methods/woopayments/test/index.js`.
- Root cause: Native Blocks correctly matched the reference by creating card payment methods through platform Stripe when `forceNetworkSavedCards=true`, but native server-side WCPay requests did not include the reference `is_platform_payment_method` contract. Local WPCOM then prefetched the platform PM as if it were connected-account scoped and failed before PaymentIntent creation with `wcpay_bad_request`. The fix introduces a provider-owned `WooPaymentsPlatformPaymentMethodContext` so this request contract can be reused by other WooPayments-provided PMs instead of living in card or WooPay UI code.
- Browser/e2e evidence: DevTools MCP confirmed target checkout test mode and `forceNetworkSavedCards=true`, created platform PM `pm_1TjLDNJ5cIRIG92xkgmsHMMr` through the platform publishable key, and submitted the Blocks Store API checkout from the live checkout page with `wcpay-is-platform-payment-method="true"`. The Store API returned 200 and produced order 167 `processing`, subscription 168 `active`, intent `pi_3TjLDiJd67Ti1EoI095GfkWo`, charge `ch_3TjLDiJd67Ti1EoI0gc9ZjtG`, customer `cus_UimnhBzJAoVe9r`, and connected cloned PM `pm_1TjLDhJd67Ti1EoIFzlkmSmo`. The shopper order-received page loaded and was captured in `data/target-devtools-order-received-167.png`.

Product-page ECE remains fail-closed for a dedicated follow-up because the reference isolated-cart/session/product compatibility contract is larger than this slice.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsExpressPaymentMethodTypes.php`; `WooPaymentsExpressCheckoutService.php`; `WooPaymentsExpressCheckoutController.php`; `NativeWooPaymentsGateway.php`; `WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapter.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/client/legacy/js/frontend/woopayments-express-checkout.js`; `plugins/woocommerce/client/blocks/assets/js/extensions/payment-methods/woopayments/express-checkout/index.js`.
- Tests: `WooPaymentsExpressCheckoutServiceTest.php`; `WooPaymentsExpressCheckoutControllerTest.php`; `NativeWooPaymentsGatewayTest.php`; `WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest.php`; classic `frontend/test/woopayments-express-checkout.js`; Blocks `assets/js/extensions/payment-methods/woopayments/express-checkout/test/index.js`.
- Root cause fixed: native ECE and native platform requests were card-only after H18. Reference WooPayments derives ECE Stripe method types server-side and validates submitted method types before request creation, while native hardcoded `payment_method_types => [card]` and frontend disabled Amazon. Native now maps configured/eligible payment request to `card`, maps eligible Amazon to `amazon_pay`, validates submitted method types against account/settings/currency/tax/context gates, and passes the validated list into charge/setup-intent request data while preserving confirmation-token-only request shape.
- Late source correction: runtime inspection showed both reference and target stores do not persist `upe_available_payment_methods`; availability is derived from account capability/fees and enabled-method settings. Native was corrected to use `upe_enabled_payment_method_ids` plus cached account `amazon_pay_payments` capability and `amazon_pay` fee data, treating an explicit `upe_available_payment_methods` setting as an optional additional gate only when present. Native also filters localized `enabled_methods` so `amazon_pay` is not exposed unless `payment_method_types` includes `amazon_pay`; classic and Blocks frontend guards enforce the same relationship.
- Review fixes: the provider helper now validates the requested context only instead of merging cart and checkout settings; `pay_for_order` maps to checkout settings while preserving the pay-for-order context for tax and request gates; the native adapter reads the submitted express context from provider data instead of hard-coding checkout; pay-for-order ECE uses order currency for localization; Amazon billing-address tax exclusion is gated by `wc_tax_enabled()`; and classic ECE initializes for Amazon-only configurations.

### 2026-06-18 H24 N7a Auth/Capture Financial Matrix

- Canonical stage: N7a widened financial reconciliation coverage for WooPayments authorization/capture money paths before A4/A5 baseline progression.
- Status: implemented, verified locally on reference and target, reviewed, and committed.
- Scope: Restore native manual-capture PaymentIntent request parity, persist authorized PaymentIntent references for later capture/cancel, defensively support `_intent_id` fallback for already-created authorizations, and widen the ignored local harness to create and capture deterministic manual authorizations on both stores.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/OrderPaymentLifecycleService.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapter.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/OrderPaymentLifecycleServiceTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest.php`.

- Root cause fixed: native PaymentIntent creation did not send `capture_method`, so customer-initiated checkout with WooPayments manual capture enabled still created automatically captured intents. The first live target manual-capture fixture also exposed that authorized lifecycle events moved the order on hold without storing the PaymentIntent as the WooCommerce transaction id, while native capture/cancel looked up only `get_transaction_id()`. Native now sends `manual` only for non-renewal checkout, keeps scheduled renewals automatic like the reference extension, stores the authorized payment reference as the transaction id, and falls back to `_intent_id` for pre-fix authorized orders.
- Runtime evidence: target native order 198 passed `financial-reconcile.sh` before capture with captured amount `0 usd`, PaymentIntent `requires_capture`, no refund/dispute/multi-currency drift, then captured through `flow-drive.sh capture --deterministic` and passed again with captured amount `5000 usd`, fee `175 usd`, and net `4825 usd`. Reference order 501 passed the same pre/post-capture reconciliations through the WooPayments extension. Stripe CLI use was read-only raw-source verification for the connected test accounts.
- Gates passed: RED/GREEN focused manual-capture request test; RED/GREEN scheduled-renewal automatic-capture regression; RED/GREEN authorized transaction-id and `_intent_id` fallback tests; broader PHP `OrderPaymentLifecycleServiceTest|WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest|PaymentProcessingServiceTest|NativeWooPaymentsGatewayTest|WooPaymentsApiClientTest` passed after review fixes with 125 tests and 626 assertions; `bash tools/woopayments-merge/tests/financial-reconcile-fixtures.sh` passed; `bash tools/woopayments-merge/tests/compare-measured-gates-fixtures.sh` passed; harness syntax checks passed for the changed driver files; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:php:changes`, production PHPStan for touched production PHP, branch `lint:changes:branch`, and `git diff --check` passed.

Local timing remains a smoke signal only for large regressions and was not used to justify this slice.

Dispute outcome ingestion remains a product gap, payout linkage remains a driver/product-disposition gap, and target multi-currency converted-money fixtures remain open.

### 2026-06-18 H26 N7a Provider-Created Dispute E2E Coverage

### 2026-06-18 H29 Provider Refund Webhook Parity

- Canonical stage: A2/A5 provider event/order-effect continuity and N7a refund-money side-effect coverage before A4/A5 baseline progression.
- Status: implemented, review-fixed, verified locally, and committed. Source/tests were committed as `443a879b66` (`fix(payments): handle native WooPayments refund webhooks`), retry hardening as `eb6e0269d8` (`fix(payments): harden native refund webhook retries`), and changelog as `14d1e4b5fe` (`chore(payments): add refund webhook changelog`).
- Scope: Restore native handling for WooPayments `charge.refunded` and `charge.refund.updated` provider webhooks without moving WooPayments refund payload semantics into the generic lifecycle abstraction. Preserve local WC refund creation/deletion, WooPayments refund meta/status, merchant notes, split-UPE ownership, explicit multi-currency note formatting, shared refund locks, and fail-closed validation for unsafe or malformed refund events.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsEventIngestor.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsRefundEventHandler.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsEventIngestorTest.php`.
- Root causes fixed: native cutover still treated refund events as known-unhandled even though the WooPayments extension mutates order refund state from those webhooks. Native now removes only `charge.refunded` and `charge.refund.updated` from `KNOWN_UNHANDLED_EVENT_TYPES` and dispatches them to a WooPayments-provider handler; account, invoice, and `wcpay.notification` events remain cutover blockers. The handler creates full refunds with line items, partial refunds without synthetic line items, stores `_wcpay_refund_id`, `_wcpay_refund_transaction_id`, and `_wcpay_refund_status`, deletes failed/canceled local refunds with `woocommerce_refund_deleted`, writes succeeded metadata only for matched refunds, and fails closed for unsafe order/amount/status payloads.
- Review fixes: the first review found split-UPE gateway IDs were rejected and explicit multi-currency note formatting was missing; both were fixed with regressions. The adversarial follow-up found stale pending duplicate retries could downgrade successful refund status and missing/malformed `captured` failed open as an uncaptured no-op; both were fixed with RED/GREEN coverage.
- Gates passed: focused `WooPaymentsEventIngestorTest` passed with 64 tests and 239 assertions; broad native payment gate `WooPaymentsEventIngestorTest|WooPaymentsProviderGatewayAdapterTest|PaymentProcessingServiceTest|WooPaymentsWebhookRestControllerTest|WooPaymentsCutoverControllerTest|OrderPaymentStoreTest` passed with 150 tests and 639 assertions, then a fresh closeout rerun of the same broad gate passed again with 150 tests and 639 assertions after the scratchpad updates; PHP syntax passed for touched PHP; direct PHPCS passed for the handler and ingestor test; production PHPStan passed for `WooPaymentsRefundEventHandler.php` and `WooPaymentsEventIngestor.php`; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:php:changes`, `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce changelog validate`, post-commit `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:changes:branch`, and `git diff --check` passed. Changelog validation still prints existing PHP 8.4 dependency deprecation noise; branch JS lint still emits existing ignored-file warnings only.
- Performance disposition: no frontend asset, bundle, or runtime timing claim is made for H29. The reliable evidence is deterministic PHPUnit/static analysis around backend webhook side effects; local timing remains reserved for large-delta smoke in the dedicated N7c gates.
- Remaining provider-event status: refund provider events are no longer a cutover blocker, but provider-event cutover is not globally green. `account.*`, `invoice.*`, and `wcpay.notification` remain intentionally known-unhandled and must be handled or explicitly dispositioned in their canonical A2/A5 slices before A4/A5 can use the baseline as known-good.
- Commit: git range `0d527ea115...14d1e4b5fe`.

- Tracked, non-blocking before A4: payout per-order membership remains an honest local/provider observability limitation because Stripe raw source does not expose fresh manual payout membership deterministically; isolated REST route-registration timing remains unverified because both stores report REST preinitialized before WP-CLI eval starts; remaining multi-currency analytics/switcher/setup asset dispositions carry into their canonical stage gates; target deterministic money drivers and WC Subscriptions renewal/email parity stay in the standing N5/A5 evidence checklist rather than blocking A4 start.

- Decision: stop extending N7 hardening slices solely to close residual measurement limitations. The next canonical implementation stage is A4 native WooPayments merchant/admin surfaces, and A4 exit must run the standing N5 spec-conformance gate against the baseline recorded here.

### 2026-06-18 A5a Cutover Preflight Closeout

- Canonical stage: A5 cutover readiness, bounded preflight closeout slice. This flips native admin surface readiness only after the A4h/N10 admin-route exit gate, preserves the last two operational queue hooks, and keeps the legacy Stripe Billing data-safety guard fail-closed.
- Status: implemented, verified, and committed as `b176692830` (`fix(payments): preserve cutover queue readiness`) plus `f5bf791694` (`chore(payments): add cutover readiness changelog`). The product diff preserves `wcpay_instant_deposit_reminder`, `wcpay_post_kyc_activation_email_send`, account-refresh Instant Deposit and KYC-completion producers, the WooCommerce post-KYC email registry entry/templates, and a bounded HPOS/CPT direct-query Stripe Billing guard for cutover preflight.
- Scope: `WooPaymentsOperationalQueueService` now owns the preserved Instant Deposit reminder, post-KYC scheduling/sending, CTA tracking, and email-class registration only when native owns runtime. `WooPaymentsCutoverController` defaults native admin surfaces ready after A4h and defaults pending operational queue hooks to empty. `WooPaymentsLegacySubscriptionsGuard` no longer hydrates orders during preflight and instead checks known legacy marker keys directly in HPOS and CPT storage.
- Review fixes: architecture review found the first post-KYC email path bypassed the WooCommerce email registry by constructing a fallback email; fixed by bailing when `WC()->mailer()->get_emails()` omits or replaces the preserved email key. Reliability review found the first implementation preserved only the post-KYC consumer and missed the account-refresh producer for `wcpay_kyc_completion_date`; fixed by mirroring the reference live/payments-enabled/non-test-drive/no-overwrite KYC-completion recording path. Final reliability re-review approved with critical 0, high 0, medium 0.
- Runtime evidence: target WP-CLI hook probe returned native runtime enabled, plugin runtime inactive, and all A5a callbacks registered at priority 10. `WooPaymentsProvider::can_process_payments()` returned true. The first full preflight probe timed out at 20 seconds because the legacy Stripe Billing guard fell through to high-level order APIs on a clean target store; after the direct HPOS/CPT guard fix, target preflight returned `preflight_failures: []` in about 1.05 seconds.

### 2026-06-18 A5b Cutover Financial Migration Readiness

- Canonical stage: A5 cutover readiness, financial migration handoff slice. This preserves the WooPayments canceled-authorization fee remediation queue as a native-owned post-cutover financial migration, fail-closed when affected data exists and native cannot schedule the queue.
- Status: implemented, verified, and committed as `c7797478c8` (`fix(payments): preserve cutover financial remediation`) plus `3679904d57` (`chore(payments): add financial remediation changelog`). The product diff adds native cutover preflight/scheduling for `wcpay_remediate_canceled_authorization_fees`, keeps the preserved dry-run/live action and option identities, and prevents plugin deactivation unless native can either prove no affected orders exist or schedule the live remediation job.
- Scope: `WooPaymentsCanceledAuthorizationFeeRemediationService` now owns `can_schedule_cutover_remediation()` and `ensure_scheduled()`, resets/cancels dry-run state before live cutover lookup, preserves reference-width affected-order semantics without exact payment-method filtering, returns explicit scheduling statuses, logs unavailable queue states, and treats WooCommerce Analytics stats-row deletion failures as non-fatal. `WooPaymentsCutoverController` injects the service, adds the `financial_migrations_unavailable` preflight failure, and calls `ensure_scheduled()` before `deactivate_plugins()`.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCanceledAuthorizationFeeRemediationService.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCutoverController.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCanceledAuthorizationFeeRemediationServiceTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCutoverControllerTest.php`.
- Review fixes: reliability review found a high-risk post-deactivation scheduling window; fixed by making queue scheduling happen before deactivation and by blocking cutover on `unavailable`. Ecosystem integration review found that dry-run cursors could hide work from live cutover and that exact payment-method filtering narrowed reference behavior; fixed by resetting dry-run processing state before live lookup and preserving the canceled-intent/date/refunded-or-cancelled-with-fee query contract without payment-method predicates. The explorer review also recorded later A5 follow-ups for local WPCOM connection readiness, owner user-token readiness, and V1 user-agent/transport continuity; no connected-plugin registry spoofing was needed.
- Gates passed: syntax passed for the service/controller/tests; focused PHPUnit `WooPaymentsCanceledAuthorizationFeeRemediationServiceTest|WooPaymentsCutoverControllerTest` passed with 52 tests and 144 assertions; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:php:changes` passed; PHPStan passed for the two production classes; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce changelog validate` passed with existing PHP 8.4 vendor deprecation noise; `pnpm --filter=@woocommerce/plugin-woocommerce lint:changes:branch` passed with existing ignored-file JS warnings only and PHP clean; `git diff --check` passed.
- Gates caveat: this slice is queue/data-safety readiness for the preserved canceled-authorization fee remediation. It does not claim fresh shopper checkout, browser, full `verify.sh`, refund/dispute/payout, owner-token, local WPCOM connection, or broad perf evidence beyond the listed queue/preflight tests and branch gates.

### 2026-06-19 A5c Cutover Platform Connection and Transport Readiness

- Canonical stage: A5 cutover readiness, platform connection and WCPay transport handoff slice. This closes the local WPCOM/Jetpack owner-token readiness and WCPay V1 transport-continuity gap carried out of A5b, while N12 re-blocks native admin-surface readiness until A4 feature parity is reopened and passed.
- Status: implemented, verified, and committed as `c77fed2d4c` (`fix(payments): preserve native cutover platform readiness`) plus `de3773cb89` (`chore(payments): add platform readiness changelog`). The product diff adds `WooPaymentsPlatformConnectionService`, keeps the WPCOM/Jetpack readiness boundary out of the transport interface, hardens `WooPaymentsCutoverController` platform preflight failures, changes `FILTER_NATIVE_ADMIN_SURFACES_READY` back to fail-closed by default, and preserves WCPay V1 account-update transport headers, idempotency, owner user-token auth, and retry semantics.
- Scope: Native preflight now requires a connected Jetpack/WPCOM store, local WPCOM blog ID, connection owner, and owner user token; those failures are hard platform blockers even when the broad preflight filter attempts to remove them. `WooPaymentsApiClient` uses `WooCommerce Payments/10.8.0` as the WCPay V1 user agent, sends account updates with user-token auth, strips caller/filter `idempotency_key` from request bodies and GET query params, adds idempotency only to non-GET/non-DELETE writes, stamps every attempt with `X-Request-Initiated`, retries only blocking idempotent transport failures, and does not retry local readiness/configuration errors.
- Source: `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsHttpClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsHttpClientInterface.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsPlatformConnectionService.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCutoverController.php`.
- Tests: `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/FakeWooPaymentsHttpClient.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/Api/WooPaymentsApiClientTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsPlatformConnectionServiceTest.php`; `plugins/woocommerce/tests/php/src/Internal/Payments/Providers/WooPayments/WooPaymentsCutoverControllerTest.php`.
- Review fixes: API-contract review approved after the WCPay V1 account-update path used user-token auth, exact WCPay user agent, idempotency stripping/header behavior, and non-GET/non-DELETE idempotency boundaries. Architecture review first rejected connection-readiness logic on the transport interface; fixed by moving WPCOM/Jetpack readiness into `WooPaymentsPlatformConnectionService`. Reliability review first rejected retrying local readiness errors and then requested explicit retry-budget semantics; fixed by retrying only `http_request_failed`/`http_request_not_executed` idempotent write failures, adding an exhaustion regression proving four total attempts, and documenting that the constant is retry attempts after the initial request.
- N12 handoff: `FILTER_NATIVE_ADMIN_SURFACES_READY` is back to fail-closed (`false`) by default. The earlier A4h route/registry/bundle gate is no longer treated as sufficient proof of merchant reachability or functional/visual parity. The current task list now carries a bottom item to reopen A4 after A5c for persistent admin navigation, Settings page parity, dashboard parity, SCSS parity, and a widened A4 exit gate before A5 cutover readiness can be reconsidered.

### 2026-06-20 A4af WooPay Disable Feedback Parity

- Canonical stage: reopened A4/N12 WooPay/settings residual parity after A4ae. This slice restores the reference WooPay disable-feedback contract without changing money movement, checkout, cutover readiness, or the standalone WooPayments plugin.
- Status: implemented, review-approved, locally verified, and committed locally as `7faed3e4fe` (`fix(payments): show woopay disable feedback`) plus changelog `6870a9caec` (`chore(payments): add woopay feedback changelog`). Native admin readiness remains fail-closed until the later full N12 parity gate explicitly passes.
- Scope: `WooPaymentsSettingsService` now exposes `woopay_last_disable_date` from `platform_checkout_last_disable_date`, records `platform_checkout_last_disable_date` only when native settings disable a previously enabled WooPay setting, preserves prior dates when WooPay was already disabled, and leaves the date untouched when WooPay is omitted from a settings update. The settings data store now merges full POST response fields over submitted settings so server-derived fields remain current in the same session. ### 2026-06-20 A4at Provider Route Reachability Parity

- Canonical stage: reopened A4/N12 route-reachability parity after the A5g cutover-sequence checkpoint. This is a product patch to keep WooPayments-specific admin surfaces under Core Settings > Payments while preserving plugin-era deep-link compatibility.
- Status: committed locally as source/tests `9cb43c2d03` plus changelog `781b6f80d5`, with git range `20f5128a71...781b6f80d5`. Slice selection is recorded in `analysis-a4at-next-slice-selection.md`; implementation plan is `plans/2026-06-20-core-native-payments-a4at-provider-route-reachability-parity.md`.
- Scope: native legacy redirect coverage now includes `/payments/connect`, `/payments/onboarding`, `/payments/onboarding/kyc`, `/payments/fraud-protection`, `/payments/multi-currency-setup`, and `/payments/additional-payment-methods`. Setup-era aliases resolve dynamically to onboarding when available, Overview when the connected account has admin routes available, and Settings as the safe fallback. Fraud protection lands on `/woopayments/settings/fraud-protection`. Multi-currency and additional-payment-method aliases land on the native WooPayments settings route with fragments, `/woopayments/settings#advanced` and `/woopayments/settings#payment-methods`, because `section` is a reserved WooCommerce settings query arg. Admin route availability and persistent menu registration now follow account state instead of the gateway enabled option, while the `gatewayEnabled` flag remains available to the UI. The multi-currency admin note now points to the same native Settings > Payments provider URL with `#advanced`.
- TDD evidence: RED navigation tests failed 9/9 on the expected alias/gateway/menu gaps, the serial multi-currency RED test failed on the stale WC Admin URL, and later regressions failed for connected-account setup aliases, legacy `tab` query leakage, stale `MultiCurrencyAdminNoteControllerTest` expectations, and the invalid `section=` route target before each fix. GREEN focused closeout now passes `UtilsTest|WooPaymentsAdminNavigationControllerTest|MultiCurrencyAdminNoteProjectionServiceTest|MultiCurrencyAdminNoteControllerTest` with 299 tests and 759 assertions.
- Browser/log evidence: Playwriter evidence `data/a4at-route-reachability-browser.json` passed six target legacy aliases on `http://store8889.localhost:8889`: setup aliases to native Overview for the current connected account, fraud protection to the native fraud settings route, multi-currency to `/woopayments/settings#advanced`, and additional payment methods to `/woopayments/settings#payment-methods`. Browser unexpected logs are empty after allowing known JQMIGRATE and Chrome unload permissions-policy noise; every result has `actualSection=null`, proving no reserved `section` query. Target `debug.log` was 0 bytes, and strict recent Docker-log scans found no PHP/WP diagnostics or actual 5xx status fields.
- Setup-required signal disposition: the plugin-era top-level menu badge is not ported in A4at. Native WooPayments sits under Core Settings > Payments rather than owning a plugin top-level menu, and source shows native provider/account settings already expose setup/onboarding URLs plus account readiness/"Payments need attention" state. A new Core Payments parent/provider badge would be a separate product decision, not a route alias parity fix.
- Review fixes: architecture review approved. Reliability review found stale legacy `tab` query preservation could override the destination `tab=checkout`; added RED/GREEN coverage and now drop legacy `tab` for non-report redirects while preserving reports as `report_tab`.
